# Review — 060-exec-analyze-assesses-each-loaded-rule

## Summary

Window `e0c220d4` (060's reopening, 2026-10-29) to HEAD, its base the reopen. One fresh subagent ran all five passes over the window's 35 scope entries and read the nine backend rule files whole, 284,888 bytes. The record's scope is 36: the 36th entry is this run's own `scripts/mutation/rule-sections-fences.py`, which the passes did not read and both fix rounds re-read.

- In depth: `4cc321b9`, `73e47741` and `c2d67cdc`, and the hunks `b1d319f1`, `0ce70cd0` and `be900a92` made in the entries they touch.
- Not re-reviewed: `74f6361c` and `4fc6a591`, read in depth by 008's review (`4c25aeb6`, window `d1853444` to `d348a641`); 060's artifacts and deliverable were read as they stand.
- Read whole, 17: 060's spec, plan, tasks and both scenarios; `rule_sections.rs`; both goldens; `framework/commands/analyze.md`; `docs/developing-ductus.md`; `docs/runtime.md`; `AGENTS.md`; and the five loaded rule files in scope.
- Read in part, so unread, 9: `runtime/CHANGELOG.md`, `runtime/src/schema/primitives.rs`, `runtime/src/interpreter/payload.rs`, `runtime/src/interpreter/mod.rs`, `runtime/src/schema/extensions.rs` and `runtime/src/main.rs`, each its window hunks and the functions they touch; `framework/commands/review.md`, `framework/reference/analyze.md` and `framework/reference/review.md`, each its hunks and the sections the criteria name.
- Not read, 9: the four generated command copies and the two installed references, each checked equal to its source but for the generator's substitutions and the Claude copies' tool-search line; `performance-frontend.md`, `security-frontend.md` and `framework/migrations/criterion-label-backfill.md`, whose window commits 008's review read.

Two broken contracts were fixed in the run. The rule-section reader cut a Verification at a fenced `#`-led or `**Field:**`-shaped line, which it took for a heading or a field; and, found by fix round 1's re-read, it closed a fence at a run carrying an info string or shorter than the opening one, so a markdown example nesting a fence still cut it. `FenceRun` and `Fences` in `rule_sections.rs` now read fences as `CommonMark` does, held by four tests and the nine mutants of `scripts/mutation/rule-sections-fences.py`, all killed. Two chores were fixed: the CHANGELOG names `loaded-rule-files`, and four stale plan passages state the design as it stands. Of the 27 stored decisions, 9 still stand and are carried, 6 expired, their issues gone from the files this run read, and 12 name files outside the scope and are retained.

2 fix round(s): 2 re-read, 0 diff-checked, 0 exempt, 0 not re-read.

## Observations

- bug — a rule's Verification is cut at a fenced block holding a `#`-led line (a shell, YAML or Python comment), and at a `**Field:**`-shaped line inside a fence: `parse_rule_sections` tests `parse_atx_heading` and `is_field_line` on every line before and without regard to the fence `render_verification` tracks. Driven on a fixture rule file through `discover-rule-files --all --rules --verification`: `**Verification:** Check the example:\n\n```sh\n# a shell comment\nexport A=1\n```\n\nThen the rest.` arrives as `Check the example:\n\n```sh`, the `#` line is taken as a level-1 heading that also closes the rule section and is listed as a spurious `other-headings` entry; with the `#` line removed, a `**Note:** x` line inside the fence arrives cut to `...```sh\nexport A=1`. The scenario an-assessment-sees-the-rule-it-asks-about says the Verification includes every block to the next field or heading, the data-model and CHANGELOG say a fenced block keeps its line breaks, and the one fence test (`a_fenced_block_in_a_verification_keeps_its_lines`) holds no `#` or `**Field:**` line inside the fence. No shipped rule file holds a fence, so only an adopter-authored rule file is hit, and the exec host then judges a truncated Verification. — `runtime/src/primitives/rule_sections.rs:196-346` (breaks the spec's contract) — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- other — 4cc321b9 added `loaded-rule-files` to every exec `assessSpecQuality` request, a wire-shape change a host sees, and touched no `runtime/CHANGELOG.md`; the later Statement entry (73e47741) describes only `rule.statement` and the Verification, so no Unreleased entry names the field. — `runtime/CHANGELOG.md:201-214` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
- other — plan.md, whose design record must state the design as it stands, describes four things the tree no longer has: the Verification as ending at a blank line or the next `**Field:**` (task 12 made it run to the next field or heading), `LoadedRules.assessable` as `Vec<AssessableRule>` (the code holds `Vec<AssessSpecQualityRule>`, which also carries `statement`), the per-rule builder as replacing `build_assess_spec_quality_request` with `build_extension_request`'s `assessSpecQuality` arm removed (both remain: `Walker::handle_assessments` builds every request through `exchange`, which calls `build_extension_request`, payload.rs:198), and a Trade-offs limitation that the exec walker sends one `askClarifyQuestion` per step and that a disposition task in tasks.md records it (`Walker::handle_clarify_questions` asks per open question, and tasks.md holds no such task). — `specs/060-exec-analyze-assesses-each-loaded-rule/plan.md:46,58,76,154` — **fixed** (decided by the rule)
